### [156] [Cardy random matrix theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31825)
*Marco Ambrosini,Alexandre Belin,Jan de Boer,Julian Sonner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个'Cardy随机矩阵模型'，其唯一输入是S-模不变性约束，并通过能量窗口粗粒化进行正则化，得到一个单随机矩阵的势函数；该模型在宏观尺度上重现Cardy谱，在介观尺度上呈现GUE统计，在微观尺度上分辨单个能级，并为3D量子引力的矩阵/张量模型对偶提供了计算实现的第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于最大无知原则，仅以S-模不变性为输入来构建随机矩阵理论，旨在为2维共形场论（CFT）的能谱和3维量子引力的矩阵/张量模型对偶提供可计算框架。

Method: 通过在固定宽度能量窗口上对S-模不变性约束进行粗粒化正则化，导出单随机矩阵的势函数；利用蒙特卡洛模拟验证粗粒化态密度与Cardy分布的一致性，并分析不同尺度（宏观、介观、微观）下的谱统计行为；识别出涌现这些现象的标度极限及有效Thouless时间。

Result: 粗粒化态密度在宏观尺度上趋近于Cardy分布（高于黑洞阈值的能隙之上），同时在介观尺度内呈现GUE邻近能级统计；模型展现出多尺度层级结构，并定义了由粗粒化窗口能量宽度倒数决定的有效Thouless时间。

Conclusion: Cardy随机矩阵模型成功统一了CFT谱的热力学行为（Cardy公式）与随机矩阵的普适统计特性（GUE），为未来在计算机上求解3维量子引力的矩阵/张量模型对偶奠定了基础。

Abstract: We construct and study a ``Cardy random matrix model'': a random matrix theory whose only input, in a maximal-ignorance prescription, is the constraint of $\mathbb{S}$-modular invariance. We regularize this constraint by coarse-graining over energy windows of fixed width, and obtain a potential for a single random matrix whose eigenvalues play the role of the scaling dimensions of primaries in a 2d CFT. The potential drives the coarse-grained density of states to the Cardy profile above a gap at the black hole threshold. We perform Monte Carlo simulations to confirm this behavior and find good agreement for the coarse-grained density of states, while at the same time revealing GUE statistics for nearby eigenvalues. We identify the scaling limit in which these phenomena emerge. In this limit, the model realizes a hierarchy of scales: at the macroscopic scale given by the window size, the occupations are frozen and the bootstrap constraint rigidly fixes the spectrum to the Cardy profile, while at the mesoscopic scale (inside a window) the Vandermonde repulsion rearranges the eigenvalues as in a random matrix theory. It is only at the microscopic scale of the mean level spacing that the individual levels are resolved. Accordingly, our random matrix model displays an effective Thouless time, set by the inverse energy width of the coarse-graining window. Our model provides a first step towards solving the matrix/tensor model dual to 3D quantum gravity on a computer.

</details>


### [157] [Localizing the M-Theory Path Integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31829)
*Pietro Benetti Genolini,Florian Gaar,Jerome P. Gauntlett,Jaeha Park,James Sparks*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种超对称定位公式，用于计算M理论路径积分的局域微扰贡献，并将其简化为对等变通量数据的积分；该公式给出了ABJM模型在一般挤压和化学势下的完整微扰配分函数的首个闭式表达式，并为描述黑洞的ABJM拓扑扭曲指数提供了新解析结果；同时，该方法还成功应用于非环面V^{5,2}理论，得到此前仅数值已知的Airy常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为获得M理论路径积分中局域微扰贡献的精确、解析表达式，尤其在ABJM模型及更广义背景（如V^{5,2}）下，亟需一种系统且普适的超对称定位方法。

Method: 采用超对称定位技术，将M理论路径积分的局域微扰部分约化为对等变通量数据的积分；利用相对等变推广的十一维Chern–Simons耦合定义局域化作用量，并借助M2-膜背景下的切-余切特征计算单圈行列式。

Result: 得到了ABJM模型在任意挤压参数与化学势下的完整微扰配分函数的闭式表达式；推导出ABJM拓扑扭曲指数的新解析结果；首次给出非环面V^{5,2}理论中Airy常数的解析形式；导出IIA型弦论全属型展开的等变拓扑弦常数图结果，并提取了积分关联函数。

Conclusion: 所提出的超对称定位框架不仅统一重现了所有已知场论结果，还提供了在一般参数下尚无场论解的全新解析预测，显著拓展了AdS/CFT对应中强耦合量子引力系统的可计算性边界。

Abstract: We propose a supersymmetric localization formula for the local perturbative contribution to the M-theory path integral, reducing it to an integral over equivariant flux data. Relative equivariant extensions of the eleven-dimensional Chern--Simons couplings determine the localized action, while for M2-brane backgrounds a tangent-cotangent character computes the one-loop determinant. This gives the first closed form expression for the complete perturbative ABJM partition function at general squashing and chemical potentials, and new analytic results for the ABJM topologically twisted index describing black holes. Applied to the non-toric $V^{5,2}$ theory, the same prescription yields a closed analytic Airy constant previously known only numerically. Our formulas reproduce all available independent field theory results, and provide analytic predictions at general parameters where no field theory answer is currently known. We derive an all-genus result for the Type IIA expansion which is an equivariant form of the usual topological string constant map result, and also extract integrated correlators.

</details>


### [158] [Cylinder Defect Casimir Energy and Weyl Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34529)
*Yang Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文区分了与共形表面缺陷相关的两个Casimir可观测量，并通过位移反常和局部反常约定分别确定了圆柱形缺陷在平直空间和共形缺陷真空中的Casimir能量表达式，且用自由场和全息计算进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形表面缺陷的Casimir能量及其与反常之间的关系，特别是在不同几何嵌入下的表现。

Method: 理论分析结合自由场计算与全息对偶方法，区分两种Casimir可观测量并推导其在不同嵌入（圆柱与大圆柱）下的能量形式。

Result: 得出圆柱嵌入下Casimir能量含对数项 $-d_1\log(R/ε)/(24R)$，由位移反常决定；而大圆柱嵌入下为 $-b/(12R)$，依赖于局部反常约定。

Conclusion: 两种Casimir可观测量分别对应不同几何嵌入与反常机制，其关系得到自由场与全息计算的一致支持。

Abstract: We distinguish two Casimir observables associated with a conformal surface defect. A circular cylinder embedded in flat space has a logarithmic contribution to its Casimir energy, $-d_1\log(R/ε)/(24R)$, fixed by the displacement anomaly. A great cylinder obtained by radial quantization of the ambient theory instead has Casimir energy $-b/(12R)$ in the conformal defect vacuum with a fixed local anomaly convention. Free-field and holographic calculations provide checks of the proposed relations in both embeddings.

</details>


### [159] [Nonlinear electroweak sphaleron clouds around Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31845)
*Carlos Herdeiro,Jutta Kunz,Hendrik Mennenga,Eugen Radu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper explores the interaction between Kerr black holes and electroweak sphalerons by constructing nonlinear sphaleron clouds around Schwarzschild and Kerr black holes in the probe limit, revealing a rich branch structure and a finite mass gap distinct from superradiant clouds.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To investigate the interplay between two fundamental solutions—Kerr black holes (from General Relativity) and electroweak sphalerons (from the Standard Model)—by studying how sphalerons behave in black hole spacetimes.

Method: Constructs nonlinear sphaleron clouds around Schwarzschild and Kerr black holes in the probe limit; revisits static and spinning (multi-)sphalerons in flat spacetime using measured electroweak parameters; analyzes domain of existence and physical properties; derives a closed-form leading-order Higgs solution on an exact Yang-Mills configuration in Schwarzschild background.

Result: Reveals a rich branch structure for sphaleron–black hole configurations; sphaleron clouds retain a finite mass gap and do not approach linear clouds; distinguishes them from superradiant instability clouds; obtains a closed-form Higgs solution in Schwarzschild background.

Conclusion: Nonlinear sphaleron clouds around black holes form stable, nontrivial configurations with distinctive features—especially the finite mass gap—suggesting novel physics at the interface of gravity and electroweak theory.

Abstract: Kerr black holes and electroweak sphalerons are remarkable solutions of two pillars of modern physics: General Relativity and the Standard Model of particle physics, respectively. In this work, we investigate their interplay by constructing nonlinear sphaleron clouds around Schwarzschild and Kerr black holes in the probe limit. We first revisit static and spinning (multi-)sphalerons in flat spacetime, using the measured electroweak parameters. We then explore the domain of existence and physical properties of the corresponding black-hole configurations, revealing a rich branch structure. Remarkably, the sphaleron clouds retain a finite mass gap and do not approach linear clouds within the families studied, distinguishing them from the familiar clouds associated with the onset of superradiant instabilities. We also obtain a closed-form leading-order Higgs solution on an exact Yang-Mills configuration in a Schwarzschild background.

</details>


### [160] [Quantum vacuum interaction between semitransparent plates in a Pöschl-Teller background: an exact relative TGTG formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31866)
*Jose M. Muñoz-Castañeda,Inés Cavero-Peláez,Gonzalo Sancho-Garrido*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了在单孤子Pöschl-Teller背景下，两个半透明Dirac-delta平板之间一阶量子真空相互作用的精确背景修饰TGTG公式，并研究了其位置依赖的相互作用能与力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确描述背景场中半透明平板间的量子真空相互作用，特别是处理零模可能引发的红外发散问题。

Method: 采用相对行列式方法定义相互作用能，扣除相同背景下的单平板贡献；结合TGTG公式和一维孤子势（Pöschl-Teller势）模型，分析一维及平面平板情形。

Result: 获得背景修饰的精确TGTG公式；证明排斥性平板下可归一化的零模不导致相对相互作用能的红外发散；给出相互作用能对平板位置和间距的依赖关系及相应位置依赖的力。

Conclusion: 背景场显著影响真空相互作用的结构和收敛性，相对行列式方法可有效消除背景相关发散，为非均匀背景中的Casimir效应提供严格处理框架。

Abstract: We derive an exact background-dressed TGTG formula for the one-loop quantum vacuum inter- action between two semitransparent Dirac-delta plates in the one-soliton Pöchl-Teller background. The interaction is defined through a relative determinant in which the two isolated one-plate contributions are subtracted in the same background. As a result, the energy depends on the individual plate positions as well as on their separation. For repulsive plates, the normalizable zero mode does not induce an infrared divergence in the relative interaction energy. We also extend the construction to planar plates and analyze the corresponding position-dependent interaction forces.

</details>


### [161] [Spread Complexity for Local Operator Quenches with Conserved Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31867)
*Ali Fatemiabhari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维共形场论中一类局域激发态（定义在平面、圆柱或有限温度下），通过为局域原初算符赋予独立的左右手调节器构造，产生具有净动量的激发；解析计算了前几个Lanczos系数及早期传播复杂度行为，并用数值方法获得更大Krylov指标下的系数和更长时间演化下的传播复杂度；最后将其变化率与全息对偶中下落粒子的固有动量进行比较，发现不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局域激发态在不同几何与热环境下的量子复杂度演化，并检验其全息对偶预言。

Method: 解析计算Lanczos系数与早期传播复杂度；结合数值方法拓展至大Krylov指数与长时间演化；对比全息对偶中下落粒子的固有动量。

Result: 获得了不同设置下局域激发态的Lanczos系数谱与传播复杂度的时间演化曲线；发现传播复杂度的增长率与全息对偶预言的固有动量不一致。

Conclusion: 局域激发态的传播复杂度虽可系统计算，但其动力学与现有全息对偶猜想存在偏差，提示需修正或拓展相关全息对应关系。

Abstract: We study a family of locally excited states in two-dimensional conformal field theories in different setups, defined on the plane, on the cylinder, or at finite temperature. These states are constructed by assigning independent chiral and anti-chiral regulators to a local primary operator, leading to excitations carrying net momentum. We analytically calculate the first few Lanczos coefficients and the early time behaviour of the spread complexity associated with these states. Then, using numerical methods, we obtain the coefficients reaching larger Krylov indices and find the associated spread complexity for a longer range of time evolution. We also compare the rate of change of the resulting spread complexity with the proposed holographic dual quantity, related to the proper momentum of an infalling particle in the dual bulk, but we observe a mismatch.

</details>


### [162] [Transient Coherence and Multiscale Spectral Structure after a Mass Quench in a 1+1-Dimensional Scalar Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31884)
*Gabriel Abellán,Nelson Bolívar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了质量猝变（mass quench）下1+1维自由实标量场中异常对关联的探测问题，提出一种基于Morlet小波平滑Keldysh统计关联函数的双局域观测量，并定义无量纲相对相干权重；该权重在固定动量下对初始温度具有精确不变性，源于热振幅在分子分母中的相消；文章还分析了有限时间窗口响应、与符号化有限时Wigner谱的对比、高斯延拓正性界，并拓展至de Sitter背景下的重整化Hartree近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测质量猝变后出现但不出现在最终粒子占据数中的异常对关联；发展能区分动力学相干与热噪声的鲁棒观测量。

Method: 定义基于Morlet小波平滑的Keldysh统计关联函数的双局域观测量；引入相对相干权重（超额值除以去相位关联）；解析推导有限时间窗口响应；对比符号化有限时Wigner谱；构造高斯延拓并导出正性约束；建立重整化Hartree近似以处理相互作用效应。

Result: 发现相对相干权重在固定动量下对初始温度严格无关；证明零均值小波在全实轴上可消除相对时间常数项；给出有限窗响应的显式形式及与Wigner谱的差异；导出新的高斯延拓正性界；完成de Sitter背景下标量模式的Hartree重整化，并划分振荡与非振荡区域。

Conclusion: 异常对关联可通过精心设计的双局域、有限时观测量有效提取；其温度无关性反映动力学普适性而非纯态判据；小波选择与时间窗口对信号提取至关重要；该框架可推广至弯曲时空与弱相互作用体系。

Abstract: A mass quench generates anomalous pair correlations that are absent from the final particle occupations. For a free real scalar field in $1+1$ dimensions, we define a bilocal observable by smearing the statistical Keldysh correlator with a Morlet kernel on the finite post-quench relative-time interval. Its excess over the dephased correlator gives a dimensionless relative coherence weight. At fixed momentum, this weight is exactly independent of the initial temperature for Gaussian thermal states evolved with the same free Hamiltonian: the common thermal amplitude cancels between numerator and denominator. The cancellation is not a purity criterion and is not exclusive to wavelets. We derive the finite-window response and show that the anomalous signal depends essentially on the observation window; an admissible zero-mean wavelet annihilates the relative-time-constant anomalous term on the full line. We compare this response with the signed finite-time Wigner spectrum and derive a distinct Gaussian-extension positivity bound. Exact smooth-quench occupations provide an independent dynamical reference. Finally, we formulate a renormalized Hartree extension and delimit the oscillatory and nonoscillatory regimes of a scalar mode on a fixed two-dimensional de Sitter background.

</details>


### [163] [General Relativity with Finite Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31928)
*Chawakorn Maneerat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有类时有限边界的广义相对论，施加共形边界条件（固定诱导度规的共形类和外曲率迹K），在线性化框架下分析了Minkowski、dS和AdS背景，并在欧氏签名下计算了受限于该边界的黑洞与宇宙学视界热力学及路径积分半经典近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展广义相对论到具类时有限边界的时空，统一处理不同宇宙常数符号下的边界问题，并建立共形规范系综以研究受限视界的热力学。

Method: 采用Kodama-Ishibashi形式主义适配含边界流形，分别在洛伦兹和欧氏签名下分析线性化爱因斯坦方程与引力路径积分；边界条件为固定诱导度规的共形类和外曲率迹K。

Result: 在线性化层面获得Minkowski、dS、AdS背景上各类边界的解行为；在欧氏情形下导出受限黑洞/宇宙学视界的共形规范系综热力学及路径积分的主阶半经典结果。

Conclusion: 共形边界条件为有限边界时空提供一致的理论框架，支持定义共形温度与K固定的规范系综，有助于理解受限引力系统的量子与热力学性质。

Abstract: In this thesis, we consider general relativity on a manifold with a timelike boundary of finite size, across all signs of the cosmological constant $Λ$. We impose conformal boundary conditions, whereby the conformal class of the induced metric and the trace of the extrinsic curvature $K$ are fixed at the boundary. In Lorentzian signature, we analyse the linearised Einstein equations about Minkowski, de Sitter (dS), and anti-de Sitter (AdS) space for a variety of boundaries, employing the Kodama-Ishibashi formalism adapted to manifolds with a boundary. In Euclidean signature, we study the thermodynamics of black hole and cosmological horizons enclosed by the boundary, computing the leading semiclassical approximation of the gravitational path integral, for which the boundary data define a conformal canonical ensemble of fixed conformal temperature and $K$.

</details>


### [164] [Wormhole fragmentation and the axion Weak Gravity Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32063)
*Puxin Lin,Gary Shiu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从欧几里得虫洞的非微扰碎裂出发，推导出轴子弱引力猜想（aWGC），并确定其作用量-电荷界限及阶一系数；该系数在无额外标量场时与虚距离界（IDB）一致，有额外标量时则增强；多轴子-稀释子体系下给出几何表述；满足aWGC的轴子使对应虫洞不稳定，有助于抑制其泛滥并解决因子化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决欧几里得虫洞带来的因子化问题，并为轴子弱引力猜想（aWGC）提供坚实的非微扰理论基础。

Method: 基于欧几里得虫洞非微扰碎裂为带电缺陷的过程，通过要求碎裂降低欧几里得作用量，导出轴子作用量与电荷之间的约束关系。

Result: 推导出aWGC的精确作用量-电荷界限及其阶一系数；明确了该系数在有/无额外标量场下的不同行为；拓展至多轴子-稀释子体系并给出几何表示；指出满足aWGC的轴子可导致虫洞不稳定。

Conclusion: aWGC可由虫洞碎裂机制自然导出，不仅统一了IDB等现有界限，还为理解量子引力中虫洞物理与轴子动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: We derive the axion Weak Gravity Conjecture (aWGC) from the nonperturbative fragmentation of Euclidean wormholes into charged defects. Requiring fragmentation to lower the Euclidean action fixes the axion action-to-charge bound and its order-one coefficient. Without additional scalars, this coefficient coincides with that of the Imaginary Distance Bound (IDB), while additional scalars strengthen the bound. The aWGC in the presence of multiple axio-dilaton pairs is discussed, leading to geometric representations. Axions satisfying the aWGC render the corresponding wormholes unstable, potentially suppressing their proliferation and resolving associated factorization problems.

</details>


### [165] [A backreacted bouncing geodesic in 2D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32071)
*Gauri Batra,Adam Levine,Stephen H. Shenker*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了全息对偶中有限温度关联函数在解析延拓后所表现出的黑洞奇点特征，特别是通过考虑二维稀释引力中的量子修正（如世界线对黑洞几何的引力反作用），发现这些修正平滑了‘反弹’类测地线奇点，并揭示了奇点物理以Stokes现象的形式编码在t=0处的共点奇点附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞奇点在全息关联函数中的具体表现形式，以及量子引力修正如何改变经典奇点信号。

Method: 在2D dilaton gravity框架下，通过引力反作用修正测地线，并结合最速下降法和Stokes现象分析解析延拓后的关联函数G(t)。

Result: 量子修正（尤其是世界线的引力反作用）使原本由‘反弹’测地线导致的奇点被平滑；奇点物理转而体现为t=0（及其热像）处共点奇点附近的Stokes现象。

Conclusion: 黑洞奇点的物理信息并未消失，而是以更精细的复分析结构（Stokes现象）重新编码；该结构与大c二维共形块中禁戒奇点的分析具有平行性，暗示某种普适的量子引力奇点响应机制。

Abstract: In holography, analytically continued finite temperature correlation functions $G(t)$ exhibit signatures of the black hole singularity. These take the form of singularities at certain values of $t$ whose bulk origin is a null geodesic worldline "bouncing off" the black hole singularity. We study certain quantum corrections to this effect in 2D dilaton gravity that are captured by gravitational backreaction of the worldline onto the black hole geometry. These corrections smooth out the bouncing geodesic singularities. By following the backreacted geodesic saddle in a steepest descent analysis we find instead that the black hole singularity physics gets encoded in a subtle way involving a Stokes phenomenon near the coincident point singularity of $G(t)$ at $t=0$ (and its thermal image). We point out a parallel between the structure of Stokes lines uncovered in this analysis and the structure present in the analysis of forbidden singularities in large $c$ 2D conformal blocks. Finally we make some preliminary remarks about the significance of quantum corrections in these models beyond backreaction.

</details>


### [166] [Learning Scattering Amplitudes with Transformer Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32096)
*Philip Velie,Tianji Cai,Piyush Jha,Vijay Ganesh,Aishik Ghosh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Transformer与强化学习的新算法，用于求解平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中高圈级散射振幅，通过融入已知对称性与线性关系，显著减少需先验设定的系数比例，并保证输出严格满足物理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纯Transformer方法在高圈级散射振幅求解中面临状态空间阶乘式增长、泛化能力差及无法保证物理约束等问题，亟需引入先验物理结构以提升可扩展性与可靠性。

Method: 将振幅系数求解建模为离散序列（symbology表示）到整数系数的映射；在Transformer基础上融合已知对称性与精确线性关系，采用关系传播+蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）联合策略：优先通过线性关系传播确定系数，仅在传播不足时由MCTS探索赋值。

Result: 相比纯Transformer方法，大幅降低需人工预设的系数比例；所有输出自动满足物理上已知的约束关系；成功实现向更高圈数问题的可扩展泛化，使机器学习结果能与传统解析方法直接比较。

Conclusion: 将领域知识（如对称性、线性关系）深度嵌入机器学习架构（Transformer+RL），是突破高圈振幅计算瓶颈、实现可靠物理驱动AI的关键路径。

Abstract: We introduce a transformer reinforcement learning algorithm that learns to solve high loop-level scattering amplitudes in planar N = 4 Super Yang-Mills theory. Our algorithm improves on previous transformer-only results by incorporating previously derived symmetries and relationships into the learning algorithm. This results in a greatly decreased fraction of the solution that needs to be known a priori to solve the entire problem. An additional benefit is that our algorithm also ensures that every output obeys the set of known relationships. Rather than predicting all coefficients independently, the model proposes assignments that are propagated through exact linear relations, while MCTS searches over assignments when propagation alone is insufficient. This is crucial to the generalization of machine learning approaches to higher loops, as without this, there is no way to overcome the factorially scaling of state sizes and compare to results derived via other methods. Using the symbology representation of the form factor, we frame the problem as learning a mapping between discrete sequences and integer coefficients.

</details>


### [167] [Hairy Taub-NUT solution in gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32139)
*Masato Nozawa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维N=2规范超引力中具有标量毛的精确Taub-NUT-AdS解族，这些解仅由非平凡标量场及其自相互作用势支撑，且所有规范场关闭；分析了Killing视界存在条件、证明其最多一个且必为非简并；发现NUT形变可在静态极限无视界的分支中产生视界；证明带非平凡标量毛的解无Killing旋量；指出全局结构存在闭合类时曲线；用协变相空间法计算守恒质量并建立NUT参数固定的受限第一定律；最后研究了欧氏区域（含欧氏特有解族）及螺栓与纽特的正则性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索四维N=2规范超引力中具有标量毛的Taub-NUT-AdS解的性质，特别是其几何结构、热力学和超对称性特征，并理解NUT参数与标量场耦合对黑洞/宇宙学视界形成的影响。

Method: 基于四维N=2规范超引力理论构造精确解析解；系统分析Killing矢量场与视界条件；利用协变相空间方法计算守恒荷（如质量）；研究洛伦兹与欧氏符号下的正则性及全局结构；检验Killing旋量存在性以判断超对称性保留情况。

Result: 1) 得到一类仅由标量场支撑的Taub-NUT-AdS精确解；2) 证明解至多存在一个非简并Killing视界，且NUT形变可诱导原静态无视界分支出现视界；3) 所有带非平凡标量毛的解均不具超对称性（无Killing旋量）；4) 全局结构含闭合类时曲线；5) 给出协变相空间定义的质量并建立NUT参数固定的受限第一定律；6) 完整刻画欧氏区域中的螺栓与纽特正则性条件。

Conclusion: 标量毛能显著改变Taub-NUT-AdS时空的结构与物理性质，尤其在视界生成与热力学行为方面；尽管引入标量场，解仍无法恢复超对称性；NUT电荷与标量场的协同效应揭示了新型几何相变机制，但全局因果性问题依然存在；该工作为理解量子引力中NUT物理与标量场作用提供了新基准。

Abstract: We present and study a family of exact Taub-NUT-AdS solutions with scalar hair in four-dimensional ${\cal N}=2$ gauged supergravity. The solutions are supported solely by a nontrivial scalar field and its self-interaction potential, with all gauge fields switched off. We determine the conditions under which Killing horizons exist and demonstrate that the spacetime admits at most one Killing horizon, and whenever it exists it is necessarily nondegenerate. In particular, the NUT deformation can give rise to a horizon in a branch whose static limit is horizonless. We also establish that the solutions with nontrivial scalar hair admit no Killing spinors. As in ordinary Taub-NUT-AdS spacetime, the global structure is afflicted by closed timelike curves. We compute the conserved mass using the covariant phase space method and formulate a restricted first law with the NUT parameter held fixed. Finally, we study the Euclidean sector, including an exceptional family specific to Euclidean signature, and determine the regularity conditions for bolts and nuts.

</details>


### [168] [Higher-Form Description of Chiral Soliton Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32212)
*Yutaka Ookouchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高形式规范场（two-form C₂ 和 three-form A₃）的新型手性孤子晶格（CSL）描述框架，通过Stückelberg耦合实现，自然包含弦与膜作为延展源，并导出与Sine-Gordon模型精确一致的晶格间距、稀疏壁相互作用、能量极小条件及无隙声子模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上CSL被描述为紧致标量场的周期性缠绕构型，但难以直接处理拓扑延展对象（如弦和膜）；本文旨在建立一个能自然纳入这些对象并保持高形式规范不变性的新理论框架。

Method: 构建基于C₂与A₃的Stückelberg耦合的高形式规范理论；推导其二次对偶有效理论以获得壁间Yukawa排斥力；进一步推广至非线性多支路高形式理论，重现CSL关键物理性质。

Result: 1) 高形式框架下导出的CSL晶格间距与Sine-Gordon精确解高度吻合（无需参数拟合）；2) 非线性理论准确复现稀疏壁相互作用系数、周期结构、能量极小条件及无隙声子；3) 声子被识别为高形式背景的集体位移，标量缠绕被重新解释为非线性高形式理论中的支路流。

Conclusion: 高形式规范理论为CSL提供了更本征、几何清晰且可推广的描述，将拓扑延展源、规范对称性与低能集体激发统一于同一框架，有望拓展至其他拓扑有序相。

Abstract: The chiral soliton lattice (CSL) is conventionally described as a periodic winding configuration of a compact scalar field. In this paper, we formulate the CSL in a higher-form framework based on a Stückelberg coupling between a two-form gauge field $C_2$ and a three-form gauge field $A_3$, in which strings and walls can be treated directly as extended sources. Higher-form gauge invariance requires the string worldsheet to be the boundary of the wall worldvolume. In the quadratic dual effective theory, exchange of the massive higher-form mode generates a repulsive Yukawa interaction between parallel walls. With its coefficient fixed by duality, the resulting CSL lattice spacing agrees well with the exact Sine--Gordon result without parameter fitting. We then extend the description to a nonlinear, multi-branch higher-form theory. It reproduces the exact dilute wall--wall interaction coefficient, the periodic CSL and its energy-minimization condition, as well as the gapless CSL phonon. The phonon is identified as a collective displacement of the periodic higher-form background, while scalar winding is reinterpreted as branch flow of the nonlinear higher-form theory.

</details>


### [169] [Rational Probes of Spectral Geometry in Hermitian Matrix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32497)
*Ali Nassar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Cauchy核和Pick矩阵的有限平面诊断方法，用于分析Hermitian单矩阵模型的平面环方程中低阶矩的约束问题；通过‘修饰鞍点’和填充分数流形的几何结构，揭示了矩空间中局部一致性条件如何先于全局平衡条件约束低阶矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面环方程无法唯一确定低阶矩，Hankel正定性虽提供约束但不能刻画多割族在矩空间中的嵌入方式，需更精细的局部谱诊断工具。

Method: 引入Cauchy核f_n(x)=(z_n−x)^{−1}作为谱探针，构造其Gram矩阵——Pick矩阵P_{mn}；利用谱判别式的非分支双重零点（即‘修饰鞍点’）定义矩空间中填充分数流形的法向约束，并以P(z,z)及其关于矩向量的梯度∇_m P(z,z)作为谱响应与矩空间敏感度。

Result: 证明在正则实修饰鞍点附近，矩空间敏感度以η^{−2}增强且沿填充分数流形的余法向方向；多个独立鞍点的响应可重构余法空间及切空间；结合Pick正定性、解析性与实性，重新导出了四次与六次模型中已知的平面结果。

Conclusion: Pick正定性提供了比Hankel条件更精细的局部矩约束机制，揭示了在全局周期匹配与变分平衡选择之前，局域谱一致性已对低阶矩施加关键几何限制。

Abstract: The planar loop equations of a Hermitian one-matrix model leave finitely many low moments undetermined. Hankel positivity constrains these moments but does not reveal how a fixed multicut family is embedded in moment space. We introduce a finite-plane diagnostic using the Cauchy kernels $f_n(x)=(z_n-x)^{-1}$ which we use as spectral probes. Their Gram matrix is the positive-semidefinite Pick matrix $P_{mn}$, whose positivity defines a finite-node bootstrap analogous to the Hankel bootstrap.
  For a fixed regular multicut topology, the nonbranching double zeros of the spectral discriminant, which we call \emph{dressed saddles}, impose transverse constraints on the corresponding moment-space locus, while its remaining directions are filling fractions. Writing $z=E+iη$, $E$ selects a spectral region and $η>0$ sets a continuous resolution scale. We use $P(z,z)$ as a finite-plane spectral response and interpret $\nabla_{\mathbf m}P(z,z)$ as a moment-space susceptibility. Near a regular real dressed saddle, this susceptibility is enhanced as $η^{-2}$ and aligns with a conormal to the filling-fraction manifold, whereas the leading enhancement cancels along tangent deformations. Responses near several independent saddles can therefore reconstruct the conormal space and, through their common kernel, its tangent space.
  Combining Pick positivity with reality and analyticity of the resolvent, we obtain alternative analytic derivations of known planar results in quartic and sextic models. The asymmetric quartic makes the one-dimensional filling-fraction geometry explicit, while sextic models exhibit independent conormal directions. These results clarify how local consistency conditions constrain the low moments before global period matching and the equilibrium variational inequality select the equilibrium measure.

</details>


### [170] [Greybody Factors and Hawking Spectra of Quantum-Corrected Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32702)
*Moslem Shafiee,Ahmad Sheykhi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了考虑真空极化和共形反常效应的量子修正Schwarzschild黑洞的霍金辐射，发现其几何具有双视界、非单调霍金温度（在临界质量处为零）及对数熵修正；量子修正抑制低能灰体因子而增强高能灰体因子；晚期蒸发阶段温度下降显著抑制辐射，导致高自旋模式可忽略，标量s=l=0模主导，并使黑洞寿命大幅延长，最终趋于稳定的极值残余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子效应（真空极化与共形反常）如何影响 Schwarzschild 黑洞的霍金辐射特性及演化终态。

Method: 基于量子修正的 Schwarzschild 黑洞度规（含双视界、非单调温度、对数熵修正），计算各类无质量玻色场（不同自旋与角动量）的灰体因子、辐射谱与发射率。

Result: 量子修正抑制低能灰体因子、增强高能灰体因子；晚期蒸发中温度下降强烈抑制辐射，高自旋模式可忽略，标量 s=l=0 模主导；黑洞寿命显著延长，渐近趋于稳定极值残余。

Conclusion: 量子修正显著改变黑洞辐射行为与演化路径，支持稳定极值残余作为黑洞蒸发终点的可能性。

Abstract: We investigate Hawking radiation of a quantum-corrected Schwarzschild black hole with vacuum polarization and conformal anomaly effects. The corrected geometry has two horizons, a non-monotonic Hawking temperature that vanishes at a critical mass, and a logarithmic entropy correction. We compute greybody factors, spectra, and emission rates for massless bosonic fields of various spins and angular momenta. Quantum corrections suppress low-energy greybody factors and enhance high-energy ones. During late evaporation, where quantum influences become significant, the decreasing temperature strongly suppresses emission. In this case, the higher-spin modes become negligible, and the scalar $s=l=0$ mode dominates. The black hole lifetime is greatly extended as it asymptotically approaches a stable extremal remnant.

</details>


### [171] [Nonlinear pole topology of slow currents in holographic probe-brane metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32748)
*Yan Han,Xiang-Qian Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了低频Drude响应与电流携带慢矢量之间的关系，通过分类三次滞后响应的极点拓扑结构，分析了非线性构成、弛豫及与其他慢模耦合对响应的影响，并利用全息方法计算了不同Lifshitz背景中Dirac-Born-Infeld探针膜的三阶谐波响应，揭示了极点拓扑的动力学选择机制及非线性输运特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低频Drude响应本身不能唯一确定哪个近守恒扇区承载电流，需深入理解非线性响应中的极点结构及其物理起源。

Method: 基于对称性和慢模式动力学，分类三次滞后响应的极点拓扑；结合全息对偶，计算有限密度Dirac-Born-Infeld探针膜在Lifshitz黑洞背景下的复数三阶谐波响应；解析非线性直流解以提取标度函数和交叉场。

Result: 在z=2边缘情况中红外响应趋近纯弛豫拓扑（p_IR≈0.994），存在归一化无关零点；z=1和z=3/2则呈现构成–弛豫混合权重；获得z=2下非线性交叉场E_nl∝T^{3/2}及参数无关稠密极限标度函数。

Conclusion: 响应的极点拓扑由系统动力学决定，而非DBI作用量形式预先设定；结果统一了高形式慢流、非线性响应理论与全息输运，并为含慢能量或形变模的扩展提供了新极点结构预言。

Abstract: Low-frequency Drude response does not by itself determine which nearly conserved sector carries an electric current. We classify the leading cubic retarded response of an isolated, inversion-symmetric slow vector by its pole topology. Constitutive nonlinearity produces only incoming-frequency poles, whereas nonlinear relaxation adds a propagator at the emitted frequency; coupling to a second scalar slow mode generates pair-frequency poles. We then compute the full complex third-harmonic response of finite-density Dirac--Born--Infeld probe branes in Lifshitz black-brane backgrounds. In the marginal $z=2$ theory, the dense infrared response approaches the relaxation topology, with a signed infrared weight $p_{\rm IR}=0.994\pm0.002$ and a normalization-independent zero at $ω_\times/Γ_J=0.2918$. Nonmarginal $z=1$ and $z=3/2$ backgrounds instead realize mixed constitutive--relaxation weights, demonstrating that the topology is selected dynamically rather than imposed by the DBI square root. The exact nonlinear dc solution further yields a parameter-free dense-limit scaling function and a crossover field $E_{\rm nl}\propto T^{3/2}$ at $z=2$. These results connect emergent higher-form slow currents, nonlinear response theory, and holographic transport, while the two-mode extension identifies the additional pole structures expected when energy or deformation modes remain slow.

</details>


### [172] [3d Lovelock gravity and the holographic c-theorem: Proof at all orders and resummation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32866)
*Gokhan Alkac,Luis Guajardo,Hikmet Ozsahin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维Lovelock引力（由正则化Lovelock不变量导出的Horndeski理论）在任意耦合参数和所有阶数下均满足全息c定理；通过构造单调c函数并验证其单调性，结合null能量条件与标量场方程的全微分性质，建立了该理论的holographic RG流描述；结果表明对偶理论是尺度不变但非共形的，并给出了中心荷（球面上作用量对数发散系数）的精确与渐近表达式，且其线性依赖于耦合常数；最后以生成函数形式给出可解黑洞解对应的重求和拉氏量、c函数与中心荷，并按有效牛顿常数正定性和真空唯一性对理论进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建满足全息c定理的高阶引力理论，拓展AdS/CFT对应至更一般的Horndeski类理论，并理解尺度不变但非共形对偶理论的结构。

Method: 利用三维正则化Lovelock不变量构造Horndeski标量-张量理论；在domain wall背景的minisuperspace中分析标量场运动方程，发现其为全导数形式；结合null能量条件构造单调c函数；计算球面上壳作用量的对数发散项以定义中心荷；对Gauss-Bonnet情形精确求解，对高阶项作渐近分析；用生成函数实现耦合常数的重求和。

Result: 证明三维Lovelock-Horndeski理论存在全息c定理；构造出显式的单调c函数；得出中心荷线性依赖于耦合常数且等于c函数的UV值；给出可解黑洞对应理论的闭式重求和拉氏量、c函数和中心荷；完成按有效牛顿常数正定性与真空唯一性的理论分类。

Conclusion: 三维Lovelock-Horndeski引力提供了一个兼具高阶修正、全息单调性与可控对偶结构的理想框架；其对偶理论虽破缺特殊共形对称性但仍具尺度不变性；中心荷作为关键可观测量，完整编码了RG流信息与理论紫外性质。

Abstract: We prove that 3d Lovelock gravity, the Horndeski theory obtained from the regularized Lovelock invariants in three dimensions, admits a holographic c-theorem at all orders and for arbitrary values of the couplings. In the minisuperspace of domain wall solutions, the equation of the Horndeski scalar is a total derivative at every order, which locks the derivative of the scalar to that of the warp factor along the flows we consider and allows the null energy condition to be integrated into a monotonic c-function. The linear scalar profile of the resulting vacuum breaks the special conformal transformations, so the dual theory is scale-invariant but not conformal, and the central charge is defined as the coefficient of the logarithmic divergence of the on-shell action on the sphere. We compute it exactly for the Gauss-Bonnet case and asymptotically for the full tower, and find that it is linear in the couplings and matches the UV value of the c-function. Since all results depend on the couplings only through two generating functions, we give the resummed Lagrangians, c-functions and central charges in closed form for the choices of couplings that lead to regular black holes, and classify the resummed theories by the positivity of the effective Newton constant on the vacuum and the uniqueness of the vacuum with a non-trivial scalar.

</details>


### [173] [Charged regular AdS$_3$ black holes in a scalar-vector-tensor theory coupled to Born-Infeld electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32878)
*Gokhan Alkac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了三维标量-矢量-张量理论中精确、静态、圆对称的带电正则AdS₃黑洞解，通过引入非线性Born-Infeld电动力学消除Maxwell情形下的对数曲率奇点，证明了核心正则性，并讨论其作为无奇点微观熵计算平台的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Maxwell场导致的对数曲率奇点问题，构造完全正则的三维带电AdS黑洞解。

Method: 基于正则化Gauss-Bonnet耦合导出的三维标量-矢量-张量理论，引入Born-Infeld电动力学，解析求解场方程并分析度规函数与曲率行为。

Result: 获得精确静态圆对称带电正则AdS₃黑洞解；证实核心处曲率有限、无奇点；发现单 horizon、双 horizon、极值及无 horizon 正则几何等多种结构。

Conclusion: Born-Infeld电动力学可有效消除该框架下AdS₃黑洞的中心奇点，所得正则时空为研究无奇点量子引力熵提供了理想测试平台。

Abstract: We construct exact, static, circularly symmetric charged regular AdS$_3$ black hole solutions in a three-dimensional scalar-vector-tensor theory obtained from regularized Gauss-Bonnet couplings. While the Maxwell-charged counterpart of this theory suffers from a logarithmic curvature singularity at the origin, we demonstrate that coupling the action to non-linear Born-Infeld electrodynamics cures this pathology. We obtain the metric function and show explicitly the regularity of the core. Depending on the choice of parameters, the solution exhibits various horizon structures, including single-horizon, double-horizon, extremal black holes, and horizonless regular geometries. Finally, we discuss the significance of this regular spacetime as a testbed for microscopic entropy calculations in singularity-free setups.

</details>


### [174] [Accelerating systems as probes of Lorentz violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33029)
*Quentin G. Bailey,Nils A. Nilsson,Sawyer J. Star*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在局域洛伦兹破缺有效场论中Fulling-Davies-Unruh效应，通过精确量化实标量场并分析其色散关系、哈密顿量及Wightman函数，探讨了加速探测器的量子场响应；发现洛伦兹破缺导致响应非平稳且依赖测量时长与时间位置，谱特征在系数约10^{-7}量级即偏离标准热谱，并讨论了实验意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究洛伦兹对称性破缺对Unruh效应的影响，检验基础对称性破缺在强引力/加速背景下的可观测信号。

Method: 在局域洛伦兹破缺有效场论框架下，对实标量场进行正则量子化，精确处理洛伦兹破缺系数；推导色散关系、哈密顿量及质量有/无情况下的Wightman函数；计算匀加速探测器的有限时Unruh-DeWitt响应与能谱；另将洛伦兹破缺置于点粒子部分，求解恒定电场中的精确轨迹并分析标量波沿该轨迹的固有时傅里叶响应。

Result: 得到含洛伦兹破缺的标量场完整量子化结构；识别出保持整体boost对称性与仅保证单加速世界线平稳性的不同条件；发现探测器响应通常是非平稳的，其能谱在洛伦兹破缺系数达10^{-7}量级时即可明显偏离标准Unruh热谱。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹破缺显著改变Unruh效应的量子响应特性，即使极小破缺参数亦可产生可观测谱偏差，为利用加速系统检验洛伦兹对称性提供了新途径；两种建模方式（场论vs粒子轨迹）均支持该结论。

Abstract: We investigate the Fulling-Davies-Unruh effect in a local Lorentz-violating effective field theory, focusing on the quantum-field response of accelerated detectors. We canonically quantize a real scalar field exactly in the coefficients for Lorentz violation. The dispersion relation, Hamiltonian, and massless and massive Wightman functions are obtained. We determine the conditions under which the scalar dynamics preserves boost symmetry and distinguish these from the weaker conditions required for stationarity along a single accelerated worldline. The Wightman functions are used to construct the finite-time Unruh-DeWitt response for a uniformly accelerated detector. For generic coefficients the response is nonstationary and depends on both the duration and temporal location of the measurement. The spectrum, which is the resulting excitation rate as a function of the energy level difference in the detector, is obtained numerically. It shows discernable differences with the usual massive thermal spectrum for dimensionless coefficients on the order of $10^{-7}$. Experimental implications of the result are discussed. As an alternative viewpoint, we place Lorentz violation in the point-particle sector, derive the exact trajectory generated by a constant electric field, and study the proper-time Fourier response of a conventional scalar wave along the modified trajectory.

</details>


### [175] [Extrinsic Renormalization of New Massive Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33690)
*Cristóbal Corral,Rodrigo Olea,Leonardo Sanhueza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了New Massive Gravity中渐近AdS时空的重整化，利用外曲率构造边界项，在一般耦合参数下实现了有限变分和全息描述；在退化点处引入二次外曲率抵消项，处理新发散并建立双源全息框架；并通过Noether-Wald方法正确复现了多种黑洞解的守恒荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决New Massive Gravity中渐近AdS时空在退化参数点处出现的新发散问题，并构建一致的全息对偶描述，特别是对非爱因斯坦解和多源情形的适用性。

Method: 采用基于外曲率的边界项和二次外曲率抵消项进行作用量重整化；结合Fefferman-Graham渐近展开、辅助场公式及Noether-Wald形式主义计算全息应力张量与守恒荷。

Result: 在一般参数下，单个外曲率边界项使作用量变分有限且满足全息源表达；在退化点引入二次抵消项后，实现双独立源全息描述并满足修正的Ward恒等式；Noether-Wald方法成功复现BTZ黑洞、非爱因斯坦AdS波及旋转毛发黑洞的有限守恒荷。

Conclusion: 基于外曲率的边界重整化方案在New Massive Gravity中具有普适性，能统一处理常曲率、非爱因斯坦及退化真空情形，为AdS/CFT在高阶引力理论中的推广提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: The renormalization of asymptotically AdS spacetimes in New Massive Gravity is studied using boundary terms constructed with the extrinsic curvature. For generic values of the theory couplings, a single extrinsic boundary term produces a consistent holographic description, as the variation of the total action is finite and expressible in terms of the holographic source. This prescription applies to constant-curvature geometries and non-Einstein solutions with Brown-Henneaux asymptotic conditions. A suitable asymptotic expansion shows the agreement of the resulting holographic stress tensor with the one obtained from the auxiliary-field formulation.
  At the degenerate point, where the two maximally symmetric vacua coalesce, the Fefferman-Graham expansion admits an additional mode associated with relaxed AdS boundary conditions. A quadratic extrinsic counterterm removes the new divergences and leads naturally to a holographic picture with two independent sources. The corresponding responses obey a modified holographic Ward identity and determine finite conserved charges.
  Finally, the proper use of the Noether-Wald formalism reproduces the correct charges of the BTZ black hole, non-Einstein AdS waves, and the rotating hairy black hole.

</details>


### [176] [Quantization of the electroweak Volkov-Proca theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33825)
*Tudor Pătuleanu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了电弱Volkov-Proca理论，研究了对称性破缺后带电粒子在平面电磁波背景下的量子化，并推导了完备正交关系、传播子及Källén-Lehmann谱表示和LSZ约化公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在外部电磁平面波背景下对电弱理论的带电部分进行量子化，拓展Volkov方法至电弱相互作用框架，以研究强场量子效应。

Method: 基于自发对称破缺后的电弱理论，构建Volkov-Proca态，分析其全局U(1)对称性与残余平移对称性；采用Ritus方法推导完备性与正交性关系；构造离壳与在壳传播子，并关联至标量Volkov传播子；进一步导出Källén-Lehmann谱表示和LSZ约化公式。

Result: 建立了电弱Volkov-Proca理论的完整量子场论结构，包括对称性分析、Volkov-Proca态的完备正交关系、传播子表达式及其与标量Volkov传播子的联系，以及适用于强场背景的Källén-Lehmann表示和LSZ公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为强激光场中电弱过程的非微扰研究提供了理论基础，拓展了传统量子场论在极端电磁环境下的适用性。

Abstract: We quantize the charged sector of the electroweak theory in an external electromagnetic plane-wave background after spontaneous symmetry breaking, which we refer to as the electroweak Volkov-Proca theory, and analyze its global $U(1)$ symmetry and continuous residual translation subgroup together with the associated conserved charges and momentum components that label the states. We further derive the completeness and orthonormality relations for the Volkov-Proca states in Ritus form, and construct the off-shell and on-shell propagators, including their representation in terms of the scalar Volkov propagator. Finally, we derive the Källén-Lehmann spectral representation and the LSZ reduction formula for the Volkov-Proca states.

</details>


### [177] [Vacuum polarization by charged matter fields in AdS spacetime in the presence of a cosmic string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33942)
*E. R. Bezerra de Mello*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了在带磁通量的理想宇宙弦存在的高维反德西特（AdS）时空中，带电物质场的真空极化效应，分别计算了标量场和费米场的能量-动量张量真空期望值（VEV），发现其为磁通量的偶函数，周期为量子磁通Φ₀=2π/e，并通过数值绘图展示了VEV随径向距离及模型参数的变化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维AdS时空下带磁通宇宙弦背景中带电物质场的真空极化效应，特别是能量-动量张量的真空期望值，以理解其对时空结构与量子场论的影响。

Method: 分别处理带电标量场和费米场在含磁通宇宙弦的高维AdS时空中的量子场论问题，解析计算能量-动量张量的真空期望值⟨T^μ_ν⟩，并借助特殊函数表达结果；辅以数值绘图分析其随径向距离和参数的变化行为。

Result: 得到⟨T^μ_ν⟩的解析表达式，证实其为磁通量的偶函数且具有量子磁通Φ₀=2π/e的周期性；通过图像揭示了其随距离衰减及对维度、磁通等参数的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 真空极化效应在该几何背景下表现出清晰的周期性和对称性，验证了量子场在弯曲时空与拓扑缺陷共存情形下的普适特征，为高维引力与凝聚态类比研究提供了新线索。

Abstract: In this paper we revisited the analyse the vacuum polarizations effect associated wit charged matter fields in a higher-dimensional anti de-Sitter (AdS) spacetime in the presence of an idealized cosmic string carrying magnetic flux running along its core. Specifically we will consider, separately, charged bosonic and fermionic fields propagating in this manifold. Our main objective is to calculate the vacuum expectation values (VEV) of the energy-momentum tensor, $\langle T^μ_ν\rangle$, associated with both matters fields. As will see, these VEVs are even function of the magnetic flux running along the string's core, with period equal to the quantum flux $Φ_0=\frac{2π}{e}$. Because the results obtained are expressed in terms of special functions, and a clear behavior for them are not very enlightening, we provide some graphs exhibiting their behavior as function of the distance to the string considering different values for the parameters presented in the model considered.

</details>


### [178] [dS/CFT Holography including Fermionic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34073)
*Akashdeep Roy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了de Sitter空间中全息原理的若干方面，聚焦于晚期边界上对偶理论的提案，特别分析了满足狄拉克方程的费米场；通过添加适当边界项，用路径积分方法构造了Bunch-Davies真空波泛函，并验证其与正则形式所得两点函数一致；揭示了一个体狄拉克场对应边界上一对原初旋量场，且波函数系数满足共形Ward恒等式；最后检验了在引入Yukawa相互作用（标量场分别属于互补系列和主系列表示）下该全息映射的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索de Sitter空间中全息原理的具体实现，特别是晚期边界上对偶理论的结构，以及费米场在此框架下的行为与对应关系。

Method: 基于de Sitter空间的对称性，求解边界附近的狄拉克场模态；通过添加边界项修正狄拉克作用量，在路径积分框架下构造Bunch-Davies真空波泛函；将体场按类时γ矩阵本征空间投影，建立体-边对应；推导并验证共形Ward恒等式；引入Yukawa相互作用，分别考察互补系列与主系列标量场情形下的映射稳定性。

Result: 发现费米场模态在边界呈现等幅振荡衰减，类比主系列标量场；成功构造出与正则计算一致的真空波泛函；确立单个体狄拉克场对应边界一对原初旋量场；波函数系数满足共形Ward恒等式；Yukawa相互作用下全息映射在两种标量表示下均保持稳健。

Conclusion: 本文为de Sitter空间中费米场的全息对偶提供了自洽的路径积分实现，确认了体-边对应关系及共形对称性的保持，并表明该构造在含相互作用的情形下仍具鲁棒性，增强了de Sitter全息猜想的可信度。

Abstract: We investigate some aspects of holography in de Sitter space, pertaining to the proposal for the dual theory residing on the late-time boundary [arXiv:astro-ph/0210603v5], including a Fermionic field that satisfies the Dirac equation. The modes of the solutions near the boundary are found to possess equal and oscillatory fall-off, similar to the scalar field belonging to the principal series representation of the isometry group of de Sitter space. We evaluate, by suitably adding a boundary term to the Dirac action, the wavefunctional of the Bunch Davies vacuum in path integral formalism in terms of the projections of the field on the eigenspaces of the time-like $γ$-matrix. We validate our path-integral construction of the wavefunctional by reproducing the two-point function between the field operators in the vacuum state as computed in the canonical formalism. By appropriately identifying the boundary sources in terms of the projected components of the bulk field, we find that a single Dirac field in the bulk corresponds to a pair of primary spinor fields in the boundary theory and show that the resulting wavefunction coefficients satisfy the conformal Ward identities. We check the robustness of our holographic map under the addition of a Yukawa interaction considering the two cases where the scalar field in the interaction term belongs to the complementary series and the principal series representations of the isometry group of de Sitter space respectively.

</details>


### [179] [RG Limit Cycles in BKT Flows $\equiv$ Periodic Real-Time Dynamics in the Current-Current Perturbed $SU(2)_1$ WZW Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34248)
*Parameshwar R. Pasnoori*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了重整化群（RG）演化与各向异性流-流扰动SU(2)_1 WZW模型中实时间量子动力学之间的精确对应关系，通过可积费米子表示和广义Bethe ansatz构造了精确含时多体波函数，并发现周期性边界条件导致量子Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov方程；该方程的相容性约束了相互作用强度的时间演化，且在普适区域下等价于BKT RG流方程，从而将RG极限环实现为相互作用强度的实时间周期演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示重整化群流与量子动力学之间的深层联系，特别是理解RG极限环的物理实现及其与可积性和周期驱动量子场论的关系。

Method: 利用可积费米子表示、广义Bethe ansatz构造含时多体波函数，推导并分析量子Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov方程，建立其与BKT RG方程的等价性。

Result: 证明了在普适区域内，量子KZ方程的相容性条件严格等价于BKT RG流方程，从而实时间演化精确复现RG极限环行为。

Conclusion: RG极限环可在实时间量子演化中被精确实现，这建立了RG流、量子可积性与周期驱动量子场论之间的直接桥梁。

Abstract: We establish an exact correspondence between renormalization-group (RG) evolution and real-time quantum dynamics in the anisotropic current-current perturbed $SU(2)_1$ Wess-Zumino-Witten (WZW) model. Using its integrable fermionic representation, we construct the exact time-dependent many-body wavefunction by means of the generalized Bethe ansatz and show that periodic boundary conditions lead to quantum Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov equations associated with the XXZ trigonometric $R$-matrix, which corresponds to the quantum affine algebra $\mathcal{U}_q(\widehat{\mathfrak{sl}_2})$ evaluated in its spin-$1/2$ evaluation representation. Consistency of these equations constrains the temporal evolution of the longitudinal and transverse interaction strengths. In the universal regime, these integrability conditions are exactly equivalent to the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless RG flow equations upon identifying physical time with the logarithmic RG scale. Consequently, the RG limit cycles of the anisotropic current-current perturbed $SU(2)_1$ WZW model are realized as periodic real-time evolution of its interaction strengths. Our results provide an exact dynamical realization of RG limit cycles and establish a direct connection between cyclic RG flows, quantum integrability, and periodically driven interacting quantum field theories.

</details>


### [180] [Three-loop anomalous dimensions of leading-twist operators in the Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34323)
*Mrigankamauli Chakraborty,Leonid A. Shumilov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型中，利用计算机代数系统和Diophantine方程求解，在$\overline{\text{MS}}$方案下计算了领头扭度算符在三圈水平的反常维数，并借助广义理论中的涌现超对称性优化计算；随后在临界点对结果进行了包括与1/N展开对比、广义Gribov-Lipatov互易性检验及右端奇点邻域行为分析等全面研究，并以ε展开形式提取了所有领头扭度轨迹的Regge截距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型中领头扭度算符的高阶反常维数，以深入理解其共形性质、临界行为及Regge物理。

Method: 在$\overline{\text{MS}}$方案下，结合计算机代数系统与Diophantine方程解析重构，辅以广义理论中涌现超对称性的优化策略，完成三圈领头扭度算符反常维数的计算。

Result: 获得了多个低自旋领头扭度算符的三圈反常维数解析表达式；验证了广义Gribov-Lipatov互易性；通过右端奇点邻域分析，以ε展开形式确定了所有领头扭度轨迹的Regge截距。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型的强耦合临界性质与Regge动力学提供了高精度微扰依据，并展示了涌现超对称性在高阶计算中的有效优化作用。

Abstract: We compute the anomalous dimensions of the leading-twist operators at three-loop level in the $\overline{\text{MS}}$ scheme in the Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model. Calculations are done for a number of low-spin operators using computer algebra systems, and the analytical form is reconstructed by solving a set of Diophantine equations. Calculations are optimized using the emergent supersymmetry of the corresponding generalized theory. For the obtained results, we perform a comprehensive analysis at the critical point, including a comparison with the $1/N$ expansion, a check of the generalized Gribov-Lipatov reciprocity, and an analysis of the behavior next to the rightmost singularity. The latter allows us to extract the values of Regge intercepts for all the leading-twist trajectories in the form of an $ε$ expansion.

</details>


### [181] [M2 brane $S^3/\mathbb Z_k$ instanton partition function in ${\rm AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb Z_k$: 2-loop correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34777)
*Arkady A. Tseytlin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了M2膜瞬子在S^3/Z_k上的2圈修正，发现其对ABJM理论自由能的非微扰大N贡献是1圈精确的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证ABJM理论中非微扰大N贡献的1圈精确性，并理解2圈修正中零模处理与超对称分离的影响。

Method: 通过投影格林函数和集体坐标雅可比行列式处理玻色与费米零模，分析4顶点贡献与雅可比贡献的抵消机制，并依据超对称性选择零模分离方式。

Result: 当k>2时，4顶点的超越k依赖被雅可比完全抵消；超对称选定的分离下剩余有理项也消失；k=1时4顶点退化为运动方程项。

Conclusion: M2膜瞬子对ABJM自由能的非微扰贡献是1圈精确的，集体坐标积分仅影响整体归一化。

Abstract: As was shown in arXiv:2609.14497, the 2-loop correction to the partition function of M2 brane wrapping $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^1$ inside $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$ vanishes, in agreement with the localization prediction for $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS Wilson loop in ABJM theory in the grand-canonical ensemble according to the conjecture of arXiv:2505.21633. Here we study the 2-loop correction in the case of the M2 brane instanton $S^3/\mathbb Z_k\subset S^7/\mathbb Z_k$ dual to non-perturbative large $N$ contribution to ABJM free energy on 3-sphere. The 1-loop instanton contribution was shown in arXiv:2307.14112 to match the localization prediction. The 2-loop computation is complicated by the presence of bosonic and fermionic zero modes, which require using projected Green's functions and accounting for the collective-coordinate Jacobian. The Jacobian provides a non-trivial 2-loop contribution in addition to the one of the 4-vertex in the M2 brane action. We find that for $k>2$ the transcendental $k$-dependence of the 4-vertex contribution is cancelled by the Jacobian for any choice of the separation of the fermionic zero modes from the quantum fields. The residual rational term vanishes for the separation selected by the supersymmetry, under which the collective coordinates and the quantum fields form separate supermultiplets. We conjecture that the remaining integral over the collective coordinates contributes only to an overall normalization of the partition function, so it is 1-loop exact. For $k=1$ the quartic vertex contribution reduces to equation-of-motion terms as in the case of ${\rm AdS}_3 \subset { \rm AdS}_7 \times S^4$ in arXiv:2511.22306 related to $S^3 \subset {\rm AdS}_4 \times S^7$ case by a formal analytic continuation.

</details>


### [182] [Semiclassical Liouville Theory on the Real Projective Plane: A Complex Interpretation of the Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34811)
*Yu Nakayama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过复鞍点方法验证了实射影平面上Liouville理论单点函数的精确解，克服了无实经典解的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证早先由bootstrap得到的实射影平面上Liouville理论单点函数的精确解，但该解尚未经过半经典路径积分计算的检验。

Method: 采用复鞍点方法处理半经典路径积分，因实射影平面上不存在实的经典解（常曲率度规为正，而Liouville理论给出负曲率），需考虑无穷多个具有负定度规的复鞍点，并选择合适积分路径保证收敛。

Result: 在适当选取的积分轮廓下，这些复鞍点成功复现了精确的单点函数结果。

Conclusion: 复鞍点在Liouville理论和二维dS引力中具有普适作用，本工作为复鞍点方法提供了罕见的、可与精确解比对的严格检验。

Abstract: The exact one-point function of Liouville theory on the real projective plane was derived long ago from the bootstrap, yet it has never been verified against a semiclassical path-integral computation. The check is less straightforward than one might expect. On the real projective plane every constant-curvature metric is positively curved, whereas classical Liouville theory produces metrics of constant negative curvature, so no real classical solution exists. Instead, as we show, the semiclassical path integral receives contributions from infinitely many complex saddles with a negative-definite metric. Once the integration contour is chosen so that the path integral converges, these saddles reproduce the exact one-point function. Complex saddles arise in the same way in timelike Liouville theory and in two-dimensional de~Sitter gravity, and the example treated here offers a rare opportunity to test their use against a known exact answer. \end{abstract

</details>


### [183] [Holography for integrable field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34909)
*Kevin Costello,Joaquin Liniado*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于4维陈-西蒙斯理论的可积场论的全息对偶，该对偶为定义在广义卡拉比-丘流形上的拓扑弦理论，并由‘平面谱曲线’这一几何对象完全刻画；进一步将可积场论的RG流解释为平面谱曲线模空间上的几何流，并在't Hooft耦合任意阶下成立，且在一、二圈阶与已知场论结果精确吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立可积场论与全息原理之间的深刻联系，特别是为4维陈-西蒙斯构造的可积模型提供一个普适、几何化的全息对偶描述，并统一理解其重整化群流。

Method: 利用全息对偶思想，将4维陈-西蒙斯理论导出的可积场论映射到定义在广义Calabi-Yau流形上的拓扑弦理论；引入‘平面谱曲线’作为核心几何编码工具；将RG流诠释为该曲线模空间上的几何流；通过显式计算一、二圈修正验证该框架。

Result: 确立了可积场论与拓扑弦之间的全息对偶；给出了RG流的几何实现（即模空间上的流）；在一圈阶涵盖广泛模型，二圈阶成功复现主手性模型（含WZ项）及一类U(N)×U(N)靶空间σ模型的RG流。

Conclusion: 平面谱曲线及其模空间几何为理解可积场论的全息结构和量子效应（如RG流）提供了统一、精确且可计算的框架，且该框架在全阶't Hooft展开下自洽有效。

Abstract: We develop a holographic dual to the integrable field theories built from $4d$ Chern-Simons theory. The dual theory is a topological string on a generalized Calabi-Yau manifold, which is entirely encoded in a geometric object we call a planar spectral curve. We describe the RG flow for a planar integrable field theory as a geometric flow on the moduli of planar spectral curves. This description is valid at all orders in the 't Hooft coupling. We compute this flow explicitly up to two loops in many examples, and find an exact match with known field theory results. At one loop this includes a wide class of models. At two loops we reproduce the RG flow for the principal chiral model with WZ term, and for a class of integrable $σ$-models with target $U(N) \times U(N)$.

</details>


### [184] [Black holes with axion hair from cubic Riemann interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34945)
*Roberto Casadio,Roldao da Rocha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种包含轴子场与立方黎曼不变量耦合的有效引力场论，证明其不引入额外的低能自由度；该模型在黑洞周围诱导出由视界正则性决定的二次轴子荷，并影响黑洞热力学与霍金辐射，甚至可能产生符合EFT有效性的黑洞残余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个受紫外物理启发、具有高曲率修正和二次轴子毛发的可控黑洞模型，并探究其对热力学和霍金辐射的影响。

Method: 基于含轴子场与立方Riemann不变量耦合的有效场论，分析轴子场在黑洞背景下的行为，施加视界正则性与渐近边界条件，构造黑洞性解并计算度规反作用及热力学量。

Result: 轴子场在黑洞周围形成由视界正则性决定的二次电荷分布；度规反作用仅出现在EFT展开的次主导阶；黑洞热力学与霍金温度被修正，允许在EFT有效范围内存在黑洞残余。

Conclusion: 该EFT模型为研究高曲率修正下带轴子毛发的黑洞提供了自洽框架，其修正的霍金辐射支持黑洞残余的存在，且整个分析保持在EFT适用范围内。

Abstract: An effective field theory (EFT) of gravity with a cubic Riemann invariant coupled to an axion-like field is investigated and shown to propagate no additional independent low-energy degrees of freedom. This interaction generates a curvature-induced scalar profile around black holes, characterised by a secondary axion charge fixed by regularity of the axion field at the horizon, while the requirement of a regular event horizon constrains the asymptotic axion background. We construct the corresponding black hole solutions and show that the backreaction of the axion field could affect the metric only at subleading order in the EFT expansion. The modified geometry impacts black hole thermodynamics and Hawking evaporation, thus providing a controlled realisation of black holes with higher-curvature contributions and secondary axion hair motivated by ultraviolet physics. The modified Hawking temperature allows for the existence of black hole remnants at the end of the evaporation within the regime of validity of the EFT.

</details>


### [185] [Exotic gravity theory in topologically non-trivial spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35253)
*P. S. Howe,U. Lindström*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论了C.M. Hull提出的六维(4,0)超共形引力线性化理论的玻色子部分，特别是在拓扑非平凡空间中的表现，并将其与dB理论及十一维超引力的场论结构进行类比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨Hull提出的六维(4,0)超共形引力理论在拓扑非平凡背景下的玻色子结构，以深化对高维超引力及其几何本质的理解。

Method: 基于超对称场论与微分几何方法，分析该理论玻色子部分的场内容与动力学，并与H=dB理论及D=11超引力的p-形式结构作对比。

Result: 指出Hull模型粗略可视为dB理论的‘平方’，但并非所有场均可如此表示；同时简要揭示其与D=11超引力中p-形式场的潜在联系。

Conclusion: 该模型在拓扑非平凡空间中展现出丰富几何结构，为理解高维超共形引力与M理论低能有效描述提供了新视角。

Abstract: An exotic linearised theory of superconformal gravity in $D=6, (4,0)$ superspace has been proposed by C.M. Hull. In this note we discuss the bosonic sector of this model in topologically non-trivial spaces. Roughly speaking, Hull's model can be thought of as a square of the $H=dB$ theory, although not all Hull fields can be so presented. We also briefly discuss the form sector of $D=11$ supergravity.

</details>


### [186] [A gravitational realization of Courant-Hilbert deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35452)
*Osamu Fukushima,Miyabi Kurihara,Takaki Matsumoto,Kentaroh Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个二维质量引力理论，其壳上作用量完整再现了Courant-Hilbert（CH）变形，从而为包括$T\bar{T}$和根号$T\bar{T}$在内的可解应力张量流提供了统一的几何机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏一个能系统导出Courant-Hilbert（CH）变形的引力作用量，而CH变形统一了包括$T\bar{T}$和根-$T\bar{T}$在内的广泛可解应力张量流。

Method: 构造一个以相对 zweibein 特征值比 $y$ 的任意函数定义的二维质量引力理论；通过引入含 $y$ 和秩一投影算子 $K$ 的 $2\times 2$ 谱分解来重写物质部分；依次求解 $K$ 和 $y$ 的运动方程，将系统映射到 Russo-Townsend 形式的 CH 流。

Result: 该二维质量引力理论的壳上作用量精确复现完整的 CH 变形；推广了 Tolley 关于 $T\bar{T}$ 流的质量引力表述至整个 CH 变形谱系。

Conclusion: CH 变形具有清晰的几何起源，即特定形式的二维质量引力；该工作为理解可解应力张量流提供了一个统一、系统的引力框架。

Abstract: Courant-Hilbert (CH) deformations unify broad classes of solvable stress-tensor flows including $T\bar{T}$ and root-$T\bar{T}$. However, a gravitational action that systematically yields such general deformations has remained unknown. In this Letter, we construct a two-dimensional massive gravity theory whose on-shell action reproduces the complete CH deformation. The gravity sector is defined via an arbitrary function of the eigenvalue ratio $y$ of the relative zweibein. To bypass the technical difficulty of directly eliminating the auxiliary zweibein, we reformulate the matter sector using a $2 \times 2$ spectral decomposition involving $y$ and a rank-one projector $K$. Sequentially solving the equations of motion--first for $K$ and subsequently for $y$--naturally maps the system onto the Russo-Townsend form of the CH flow. Our work extends Tolley's massive gravity formulation of $T\bar{T}$ flows to the CH landscape, providing a clear geometric mechanism for solvable stress-tensor flows.

</details>


### [187] [Multi-rotating black holes with non-aligned angular momenta in 5D Kaluza-Klein theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11609)
*Shinya Tomizawa,Hideo Furugori,Jun-ichi Sakamoto,Ryotaku Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了4维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-稀子理论中多旋转黑洞的精确解，该解由5维卡鲁扎-克莱因理论经维度约化得到，推广了此前角动量共线的情形至更一般的非共线情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广先前具有共线角动量的多黑洞解，构建更普适的、允许各黑洞角动量非共线的多中心旋转带电黑洞精确解。

Method: 通过5维卡鲁扎-克莱因理论的维度约化，构造4维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-稀子理论中的多中心、非共线角动量、带电（电荷与磁荷均可不等）旋转黑洞精确解，并分析其奇点与闭合类时曲线性质。

Result: 得到了满足无曲率奇点和无闭合类时曲线（在视界内外）条件的多旋转、多中心、非共线角动量黑洞解；该解涵盖Majumdar-Papapetrou解、Teo-Wan解及作者先前的共线解作为特例。

Conclusion: 非共线角动量的多黑洞精确解在适当自旋上限约束下可保持时空正则性，为研究强引力场中复杂多体黑洞系统提供了新工具和严格基准。

Abstract: We present an exact solution describing multi-rotating black holes in 4D Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory, which can be obtained from 5D Kaluza--Klein theory via dimensional reduction. The solution represents a multi-centered configuration of rotating black holes carrying both electric and magnetic charges, with each black hole possessing a non-aligned angular momentum. This work generalizes our previous solution for black holes with aligned angular momenta to the more general case of non-aligned angular momenta. It includes, as special cases, the Majumdar--Papapetrou solution, the recent multi-centered rotating black hole solutions of Teo and Wan, and our previous solution with unequal electric and magnetic charges. The resulting spacetimes are free of curvature singularities and closed timelike curves, both on and outside the horizons, provided that the magnitude of the spin angular momentum of each black hole remains below a certain upper bound.

</details>


### [188] [Regge in Wonderland](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35455)
*Alessio Miscioscia,Dmitrii Pavshinkin,Fedor K. Popov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究共形场论中大自旋算符的渐近行为，通过将理论置于pp波背景下来分析包含两个大自旋算符和一个轻算符的三点系数，并将其映射为pp波上的热单点函数；在高、低温两种极限下分别预测该单点函数，高温度下用热有效场论推导，低温下与解析bootstrap结果匹配；并在自由标量理论和大N O(N)模型中进行了检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形场论中大自旋算符的渐进行为对探索AdS/CFT对偶、bootstrap程序及强耦合动力学具有重要意义；pp波背景提供了一个可控的曲率背景来研究自旋极限。

Method: 将CFT置于pp波背景，将三点系数映射为热单点函数；分别在高（twist ~ spin^α）和低（spin ≫ twist）温极限下分析；高温度使用热有效场论，低温下对接解析bootstrap；在自由标量理论和数值求解大N O(N)模型上验证。

Result: 得到了高、低温下热单点函数的渐近形式：高温下由热EFT给出幂律行为，低温下与bootstrap预测一致；自由理论和O(N)模型的数值结果均与理论预测吻合，包括单点函数和自由能。

Conclusion: pp波背景为研究CFT大自旋极限提供了有力框架；高/低温分析统一了EFT与bootstrap视角；结果支持共形场论在非平凡背景下的热化行为与自旋- twist分离结构的普适性。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of large-spin operators in conformal field theories by placing the theory on a pp-wave background. In this setting, three-point coefficients involving two large-spin operators and a light operator are mapped to thermal one-point functions on the pp-wave. We predict these one-point functions in the two complementary regimes of high- and low-temperature respectively, corresponding to a limit in which the twist scales with an appropriate power of the spin and the spin is parametrically larger than the twist, respectively. In particular, at high temperature we derive their asymptotic behavior using thermal effective field theory while at low temperature we obtain results that can be matched directly to predictions from the analytic bootstrap. We test these general results in free scalar theories. Finally, we solve the large-$N$ $\mathrm{O}(N)$ model numerically on the pp-wave, finding agreement with our predictions for both the one-point function and the free energy in the high- and low-temperature regimes.

</details>


### [189] [Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov equations and the analytic structure of strings in AdS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35495)
*Sergio Iguri,Nicolas Kovensky,Julian H. Toro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，通过世界面相关函数的局域化性质求解KZ方程，并显式积分世界面模空间，系统计算AdS₃中弦振幅的极点留数和完整解析结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅能处理n点函数中特定子集的极点，且依赖自由场技术；需发展适用于任意n和任意谱流荷的系统性方法以完善AdS₃/CFT₂对偶中的解析结构理解。

Method: 利用世界面相关函数的局域化性质，在对应流形上求解Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov（KZ）方程，并显式完成世界面模空间积分。

Result: 实现了对任意n点函数、任意谱流荷下剩余极点留数的系统计算，为确定AdS₃中弦振幅的完整解析结构提供了统一框架。

Conclusion: 该方法超越了自由场限制，为AdS₃/CFT₂对偶中全息CFT在非张力零点附近的非微扰结构研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: The pole structure of string correlators in AdS$_3$ encodes essential information about the AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ duality. The corresponding residues for spectrally flowed three-point functions have been extracted from their exact expressions and shown to match the predictions of the holographic CFT. Away from the tensionless point, the latter is defined perturbatively in terms of a deformed symmetric orbifold. For $n$-point functions, this has so far only been achieved for a specific subset of poles and employing free-field techniques. Here we develop an alternative approach. We show that, starting from the localization properties of worldsheet correlators, one can solve the KZ equations on the corresponding locus and perform the integration over worldsheet moduli explicitly for any $n$ and for arbitrary values of the spectral flow charges. This provides a systematic framework for computing the remaining residues and, ultimately, for determining the full analytic structure of string amplitudes in AdS$_3$.

</details>


### [190] [Symmetric Amplituhedra I: Spinor Helicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35604)
*Ran J. Tessler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了非负对称格拉斯曼流形，并在特定条件下定义了对称振幅多面体，研究其几何性质及BCFW分解。


<details>

Motivation: 推广先前关于非负格拉斯曼流形的对称性构造（如Karpman2018、Fraser2020、Shevchenko2025），并建立与物理中振幅多面体（如ABJM、反射拉格朗日情形）的联系。

Method: 利用二面体群D_N与Z_2的子群作用于非负格拉斯曼流形，通过带符号修正的列作用和正交补交换构造不动点子空间；在k∈[2,N−2]时进一步定义对称振幅多面体，并分析其结构与BCFW分解猜想。

Result: 成功构造了广义非负对称格拉斯曼流形；定义了对称振幅多面体；揭示其与ABJM及反射拉格朗日振幅多面体的对应关系；初步分析了几何性质与BCFW分解可能性。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了多种对称振幅多面体构造，为理解散射振幅的几何结构提供了新工具，并支持相关组合与物理猜想。

Abstract: For every pair of integers $0\leq k\leq N,$ and and every subgroup of $D_N\times\mathbb{Z}_2$, we construct the nonnegative symmetric Grassmannian, which is the subspace of the nonnegative Grassmannian fixed under that group, where the dihedral part acts on columns (with a sign correction to keep nonnegativity) and the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ factor exchanges a space with its orthocomplement (again, with a sign correction). This construction generalizes the earlier constructions of \cite{Karpman2018,Fraser2020,Shevchenko2025}. We study the geometry of this space. Then, when $k$ is further restricted to the range $[2,N-2]$ we also define a symmetric amplituhedron. We analyze its basic properties and its conjectural BCFW decomposition. The ABJM and reflected Lagrangian amplituhedra are the special cases corresponding to the groups $\{1\}\times\mathbb{Z}_2,$ and the group generated by a reflection combined with the exchange,

</details>


### [191] [Microscopic Organization of BPS Black-Hole States in the D1--D5--P CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35688)
*Ji-Seong Chae*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究D1-D5共形场论中的BPS态，通过其偶然性（fortuity）、循环结构及探针响应进行分析；发现循环划分不能逐态决定偶然性，但在大N极限下固定电荷计数倾向于选择长弦；在未凝聚区域存在一个携带熵的精确非引力子子空间，集中于长度为N-O(1)的单个循环；越过临界线后自由系综出现短弦凝聚；通过有限绕数探针验证该循环结构在可观测量中的体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解D1-D5 CFT中BPS态的微观结构及其与AdS/CFT对偶中黑洞几何（如极端BTZ）的对应关系，特别是循环结构、偶然性与热力学/动力学行为之间的联系。

Method: 结合组合分析（循环划分、绕数结构）、大N统计计数、熵分析、有限绕数探针的关联函数计算以及时间尺度（O(√N)、O(N)）上的动力学行为研究。

Result: 1) 循环划分不唯一确定偶然性态；2) 大N下固定电荷系综主导态为长弦；3) 未凝聚区存在熵承载的近全绕数非引力子子空间；4) 超过临界点出现短弦凝聚；5) 长循环标量关联函数趋近极端BTZ响应，不同时间尺度可分辨绕数结构与谱权重。

Conclusion: BPS态的循环几何深刻影响其统计、热力学与动力学性质，是连接CFT微观态与引力几何的关键组织原则；偶然性和绕数结构共同刻画了从无序到凝聚、从CFT到AdS的相变行为。

Abstract: We study BPS states in the D1--D5 CFT through their fortuity, cycle structure, and probe response. Explicit fortuitous states with both long- and short-string geometries show that the cycle partition does not determine fortuity state by state. At large $N$, fixed-charge counting nevertheless selects a long string. In the open uncondensed region, an entropy-carrying exact non-graviton subspace concentrates on one cycle of winding $N-O(1)$. Assuming subexponential monotone dimension, the same holds for the harmonic fortuitous complement. Beyond the critical line, the free ensemble develops a short-string condensate. To test how this cycle structure appears in observables, we study finite-winding probes. The long-cycle scalar correlator approaches the extremal-BTZ response at fixed time, while finite winding is resolved at \(t=O(\sqrt N)\), the exact circle recurs at \(t=O(N)\), and states with the same cycle geometry remain distinguishable by their spectral weights.

</details>


### [192] [Journey to the Center of a Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35705)
*Vyshnav Mohan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维BTZ黑洞中一个标量场的下落两点函数，发现当一个算符位于外部、另一个沿类时测地线插入并趋近奇点时，该函数发散；这种发散在边界理论中完全由双扭曲算符交换引起，而非高维中表征曲率奇点的多应力张量部分；引入具有Hagedorn增长的重粒子塔后，其圈修正会在奇点附近变得显著，引发Hagedorn相变，从而破坏树图级发散的可靠性；相应地，边界上对应的原初算符塔导致探针算符在黑洞态中的广义自由场描述失效；对于大黑洞，这些修正仅在奇点附近重要，在视界处仍呈指数压制，因此主导区域与视界参数分离，符合黑洞互补性原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内部奇点附近的量子引力效应，特别是边界共形场论（CFT）如何编码奇点物理，以及传统微扰描述（如广义自由场）在强引力区域的失效机制。

Method: 基于AdS/CFT对偶，计算BTZ黑洞中下落两点函数的渐近行为；分析边界CFT中双扭曲算符与多应力张量算符的贡献；引入具有Hagedorn谱的重粒子塔，计算其在体理论中引起的圈修正，并考察其在奇点附近的增强效应；结合黑洞互补性原理讨论修正主导区域与视界的分离性。

Result: 发现下落两点函数在奇点处发散源于边界CFT中双扭曲算符的交换；Hagedorn型重粒子塔引发的圈修正导致奇点附近发生Hagedorn相变，使树图级结果不可靠；边界上对应原初算符塔破坏探针算符的广义自由场描述；对大黑洞，该修正区域与视界参数分离。

Conclusion: 三维BTZ黑洞奇点的量子行为不能由树图级AdS/CFT或广义自由场描述充分刻画，需纳入具有Hagedorn谱的高维算符/粒子的非微扰效应；这一结果为黑洞互补性提供了具体实现机制，并暗示奇点物理由边界CFT中特定算符谱的整体结构决定。

Abstract: An infalling two-point function of a scalar field in a three-dimensional BTZ black hole, with one operator inserted in the exterior and the other inserted along a timelike geodesic, diverges when the infalling insertion point reaches the black hole singularity. In the boundary description, this divergence comes entirely from the exchange of double-twist operators, rather than from the multi-stress-tensor sector that encodes the curvature singularities in higher dimensions. We show that the inclusion of a tower of massive particles with Hagedorn growth gives rise to loop corrections that become important near the singularity, where they trigger a Hagedorn transition that renders the tree-level divergence untrustworthy. In the boundary theory, the corresponding tower of primary operators leads to a breakdown of the generalized free field description of the probe operator in the black hole state. In a large black hole, these corrections become important only near the singularity and remain exponentially suppressed at the horizon. The region where these corrections dominate is therefore parametrically separated from the horizon, as anticipated by the principle of black hole complementarity.

</details>
